**[00:05:37.480] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000080)

**[00:05:43.060] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000081)

**[00:05:47.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000082)

**[00:05:50.820] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000083)

**[00:05:58.900] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000084)

**[00:06:04.380] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000085)  
Whether immediately naramdaman or eventually maramdaman because we also do research.

**[00:06:10.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000086)  
Alam po natin na ang tamang paggamit ng aghama teknolohiya,

**[00:06:14.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000087)  
sa tamang paggamit ng aghama teknolohiya,

**[00:06:17.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000088)  
marami pong magagandang bagay ang pwedeng mangyari.

**[00:06:21.800] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000089)  
Pwede pong mapataas ang ani ng magsasaka at manging isda.

**[00:06:25.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000090)  
Pwedeng mapahaba ang shelf life ng pagkain.

**[00:06:28.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000091)  
Pwedeng mabawasan ang nasasayang na produkto.

**[00:06:31.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000092)  
Pwedeng mapababa ang gasto sa produksyon, sa enerhiya, sa transportasyon at distribusyon.

**[00:06:36.820] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000093)  
Matutulungan din ang malilihit na negosyo na gumamit ang teknolohiya

**[00:06:40.240] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000094)  
upang makalikha ng dekalidad,

**[00:06:43.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000095)  
ngunit mas murang pagkain, gamot at iba pang pangunahing bilihit.

**[00:06:47.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000096)  
Ang DOST po, ramdam na ramdam rin sa pagprotekta ng ating komunidad

**[00:06:51.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000097)  
laban sa baha, bagyo, matinding init, lindol, paguho ng lupa at iba pang mga sakuna.

**[00:06:58.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000098)  
Sa pamamagitan ng accurate na weather forecasting, hazard mapping, early warning systems,

**[00:07:03.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000099)  
flood monitoring and climate resilient technologies,

**[00:07:07.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000100)  
mas maagang nakapaghahanda ang mga lokal na pamahalaan at mga mamamayan.

**[00:07:12.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000101)  
Mas maraming buhay ang naliligtas at

**[00:07:15.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000102)  
inaasahan na mas kaunting tahanan,

**[00:07:17.880] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000103)

**[00:07:21.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000104)  
Ito po yung malinaw na layunin ng ating budget,

**[00:07:23.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000105)  
gawing kapakikipakinabang ang agham sa pang araw-araw na buhay ng Pilipino.

**[00:07:29.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000106)  
Ang tagumpay ng DOST hindi lamang nasusukat sa dami ng pag-aaral, patent, inventions or innovation,

**[00:07:37.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000107)  
dapat naramdaman na nito ng ating mga kababayan.

**[00:07:40.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000108)  
Sa kanilang pang araw-araw na buhay, sa palengke, sa paaralan,

**[00:07:44.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000109)  
sa kanilang...

**[00:07:45.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000110)  
kung saan sila nagtatrabaho at kung saan pa man.

**[00:07:50.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000111)  
Alam po natin na sa tamang paggamit ng aghama teknolohiya,

**[00:07:54.700] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000112)  
mas magandang buhay ang maaari nating maipigay sa ating mga kababayan.

**[00:07:59.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000113)  
Kaya ngayon po, sa pagtutok natin sa budget ng DOST,

**[00:08:03.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000114)

**[00:08:08.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000115)

**[00:08:12.980] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000116)

**[00:08:17.200] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000117)

**[00:08:22.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000118)

**[00:11:06.140] Presentation video** (asr-000173)

in areas of maximum economic and social benefits for the people.

**[00:11:25.960] Presentation video** (asr-000178)  
Within the bounds of our mandate,

**[00:11:27.640] Presentation video** (asr-000179)  
we envision to be the leading enabler and provider

**[00:11:30.100] Presentation video** (asr-000180)  
of science, technology, and innovation, STI,

**[00:11:33.600] Presentation video** (asr-000181)  
explicit solutions towards national development.

**[00:11:36.700] Presentation video** (asr-000182)  
The DOST is composed of 19 attached agencies,

**[00:11:39.740] Presentation video** (asr-000183)  
17 regional,

**[00:11:41.860] Presentation video** (asr-000184)  
and 80 provincial,

**[00:11:43.060] Presentation video** (asr-000185)  
science and technology offices.

**[00:11:45.200] Presentation video** (asr-000186)  
The DOST continues to lead the Philippines

**[00:11:48.520] Presentation video** (asr-000187)  
in developing innovative science and technology solutions

**[00:11:52.120] Presentation video** (asr-000188)  
that create transformative leaps

**[00:11:54.200] Presentation video** (asr-000189)  
towards overcoming emerging issues facing Filipinos.

**[00:11:58.400] Presentation video** (asr-000190)  
In support of the administration's socio-economic agenda,

**[00:12:01.860] Presentation video** (asr-000191)  
the policies, plans, and programs of the Department

**[00:12:05.060] Presentation video** (asr-000192)  
are intertwined in four strategic pillars,

**[00:12:08.160] Presentation video** (asr-000193)  
namely,

**[00:12:08.900] Presentation video** (asr-000194)  
1. Promotion of human well-being,

**[00:12:11.860] Presentation video** (asr-000195)  
2.

**[00:12:12.420] Presentation video** (asr-000196)  
fostering

**[00:12:12.980] Presentation video** (asr-000197)  
wealth creation,

**[00:12:14.540] Presentation video** (asr-000198)  
3. reinforcing wealth protection,

**[00:12:17.340] Presentation video** (asr-000199)  
and 4. institutionalizing sustainability.

**[00:12:20.820] Presentation video** (asr-000200)  
The Human Well-Being Pillar advances initiatives

**[00:12:23.940] Presentation video** (asr-000201)  
to improve the quality of life of Filipinos

**[00:12:26.960] Presentation video** (asr-000202)  
with the following key accomplishments achieved in 2025

**[00:12:30.060] Presentation video** (asr-000203)  
to support the implementation of water filtration systems.

**[00:12:33.360] Presentation video** (asr-000204)  
On improving access to efficient transportation systems,

**[00:12:36.960] Presentation video** (asr-000205)  
the DOST promotes cleaner and more sustainable mobility solutions,

**[00:12:41.460] Presentation video** (asr-000206)  
including the electric fairways,

**[00:12:42.560] Presentation video** (asr-000207)  
electric ferries,

**[00:12:43.060] Presentation video** (asr-000208)  
and e-tranvias,

**[00:12:44.240] Presentation video** (asr-000209)  
as well as charm and battery swapping technologies

**[00:12:47.440] Presentation video** (asr-000210)  
that support the wider adoption of electric vehicles.

**[00:12:50.840] Presentation video** (asr-000211)  
These efforts also include converted e-tricycles

**[00:12:53.960] Presentation video** (asr-000212)  
and electric tricycles,

**[00:12:55.320] Presentation video** (asr-000213)  
providing practical and environmentally friendly transportation options

**[00:12:59.320] Presentation video** (asr-000214)  
for local communities.

**[00:13:00.920] Presentation video** (asr-000215)  
On improving health and nutrition,

**[00:13:03.240] Presentation video** (asr-000216)  
the DOST advances health and nutrition through R&D in biomedical engineering,

**[00:13:08.840] Presentation video** (asr-000217)  
genomics, emerging diseases, and brain and mental health,

**[00:13:13.200] Presentation video** (asr-000218)  
These innovations strengthen disease prevention,

**[00:13:15.880] Presentation video** (asr-000219)  
early detection, treatment, and health care delivery,

**[00:13:19.480] Presentation video** (asr-000220)  
contributing to a healthier and more resilient Philippines.

**[00:13:23.600] Presentation video** (asr-000221)  
The DOST develops and transfers healthy and high-value food products

**[00:13:28.640] Presentation video** (asr-000222)  
to improve Filipino nutrition.

**[00:13:30.920] Presentation video** (asr-000223)  
By bringing these innovations to market,

**[00:13:33.000] Presentation video** (asr-000224)  
they make healthy food more accessible

**[00:13:35.320] Presentation video** (asr-000225)  
while boosting business for local farmers and food manufacturers.

**[00:13:39.320] Presentation video** (asr-000226)  
On achieving quality science education

**[00:13:41.680] Presentation video** (asr-000227)  
and enhancing health,

**[00:13:42.240] Presentation video** (asr-000228)  
and enhancing knowledge and contribution of S&T talents,

**[00:13:45.600] Presentation video** (asr-000229)  
the DOST invests in developing the country's next generation of scientists,

**[00:13:50.200] Presentation video** (asr-000230)  
engineers, and researchers.

**[00:13:52.400] Presentation video** (asr-000231)  
The DOST Scholarship Program supports learners from secondary school to graduate studies.

**[00:13:57.960] Presentation video** (asr-000232)  
For academic year 2025-2026,

**[00:14:01.080] Presentation video** (asr-000233)  
it supports 10,810 specialized science scholars,

**[00:14:05.400] Presentation video** (asr-000234)  
48,520 undergraduate scholars,

**[00:14:08.880] Presentation video** (asr-000235)  
and 5,852 graduate scholars,

**[00:14:12.160] Presentation video** (asr-000236)  
with continued expansion targeted for 2026-2027.

**[00:14:16.800] Presentation video** (asr-000237)  
The Balik Scientist Program brings Filipino scientists and experts from abroad

**[00:14:21.280] Presentation video** (asr-000238)  
back to the country to share their knowledge,

**[00:14:23.440] Presentation video** (asr-000239)  
conduct research,

**[00:14:24.560] Presentation video** (asr-000240)  
and strengthen local S&T capabilities.

**[00:14:27.520] Presentation video** (asr-000241)  
From January to June 2026,

**[00:14:29.920] Presentation video** (asr-000242)  
23 Balik scientists have been engaged.

**[00:14:32.840] Presentation video** (asr-000243)  
The Wealth Creation Pillar drives economic growth through STI,

**[00:14:37.440] Presentation video** (asr-000244)  
with the following key accomplishments achieved in 2025.

Advancing R&D and innovation.

These are the technologies developed from R&D to commercialization or spin-off.

into higher-value products, creating new opportunities for

Technologies on weaving,

craft manufacturing, food

**[00:15:06.040] Presentation video** (asr-000259)  
processing, and agriculture

help local producers modernize

fiber cycle, which converts

plastic bottles into yarn,

**[00:15:33.860] Presentation video** (asr-000273)

Together, these innovations

transform scientific research

DOST advances R&D

and innovation in artificial

Universal Structural Health Evaluation

And Vigorman, a wastewater treatment

and odor reduction formulation that

The DOST scales up technology adoption

**[00:18:36.820] Presentation video** (asr-000362)  
and commercialization with initiatives

**[00:18:39.120] Presentation video** (asr-000363)  
that turn technology into opportunities,

**[00:18:42.440] Presentation video** (asr-000364)  
creating jobs, strengthening MSMEs,

**[00:18:45.460] Presentation video** (asr-000365)  
supporting vulnerable communities,

**[00:18:47.380] Presentation video** (asr-000366)  
and driving inclusive economic growth

**[00:18:49.920] Presentation video** (asr-000367)  
across the country.

**[00:18:51.500] Presentation video** (asr-000368)  
Through the Small Enterprise Technology

**[00:18:53.700] Presentation video** (asr-000369)  
Upgrading Program, or SETUP 4.0,

**[00:18:56.840] Presentation video** (asr-000370)  
DOST helps MSMEs adopt

**[00:18:59.100] Presentation video** (asr-000371)  
appropriate technologies,

**[00:19:00.940] Presentation video** (asr-000372)  
improve productivity, and expand their operations.

**[00:19:04.300] Presentation video** (asr-000373)  
In 2025, the program supported nearly 23,000 firms,

**[00:19:09.300] Presentation video** (asr-000374)  
created over 11,800 jobs, and delivered

**[00:19:12.740] Presentation video** (asr-000375)  
more than 21,700 technology interventions.

Through the Communities Empowerment

through Science and Technology, or SEST program,

**[00:19:22.240] Presentation video** (asr-000378)  
DOST brings science and technology solutions

**[00:19:24.800] Presentation video** (asr-000379)  
directly to communities, addressing needs

**[00:19:27.400] Presentation video** (asr-000380)  
in health, education, livelihood,

**[00:19:29.100] Presentation video** (asr-000381)  
environmental protection and disaster resilience. The program assisted nearly 1,300 communities in 2025 and more than 1,000 communities in the first half of 2026. DOST also provides targeted support

**[00:19:44.480] Presentation video** (asr-000382)  
through innovations for Filipinos

working distantly from the Philippines,

**[00:19:48.440] Presentation video** (asr-000384)  
iForwardPH for returning OFWs,

**[00:19:51.340] Presentation video** (asr-000385)  
livelihood assistance for conflict-vulnerable

**[00:19:53.440] Presentation video** (asr-000386)  
communities in Mindanao,

**[00:19:54.640] Presentation video** (asr-000387)  
and the Good Agri-Aqua Livelihood Initiative

**[00:19:57.480] Presentation video** (asr-000388)  
towards National Goals,

**[00:19:59.100] Presentation video** (asr-000389)  
and the Galling-Picard-Contra-Crisis Program

**[00:20:01.560] Presentation video** (asr-000390)  
for Agricultural Recovery.

The DOST drives economic growth

by scaling technology adoption

Programs such as Expanded Venture Financing Program,

Technology for Commercialization Program,

Creativity and Equity through Accelerated

Textile Technology Engagements and Entrepreneurship

for Vulnerable Sectors,

To boost intellectual property protection

**[00:21:43.260] Presentation video** (asr-000430)  
and the Nuclear Plant Simulator and Analyzer, or NUSIM.

**[00:21:47.420] Presentation video** (asr-000431)  
At the regional level,

**[00:21:48.720] Presentation video** (asr-000432)  
Metals and Engineering Innovation Centers, or MEECs,

**[00:21:51.620] Presentation video** (asr-000433)  
are being established to bring

**[00:21:52.900] Presentation video** (asr-000434)  
advanced engineering capabilities

**[00:21:54.640] Presentation video** (asr-000435)  
closer to industries across the country.

**[00:21:57.760] Presentation video** (asr-000436)  
For high-tech manufacturers,

**[00:21:59.300] Presentation video** (asr-000437)  
facilities for Advanced Materials Testing

**[00:22:01.400] Presentation video** (asr-000438)  
and Advanced Manufacturing Center,

**[00:22:03.640] Presentation video** (asr-000439)  
AMSEN, for additive manufacturing

**[00:22:05.520] Presentation video** (asr-000440)  
strengthen local industrial capabilities.

**[00:22:08.640] Presentation video** (asr-000441)  
The DOST also strengthens STI support

**[00:22:11.740] Presentation video** (asr-000442)  
for production and manufacturing

**[00:22:13.380] Presentation video** (asr-000443)  
through the nationwide rollout

**[00:22:15.160] Presentation video** (asr-000444)  
of the Frontier Regional Textile Innovation Hubs.

**[00:22:18.760] Presentation video** (asr-000445)  
These initiatives help localize

**[00:22:20.480] Presentation video** (asr-000446)  
the textile supply chain,

**[00:22:22.100] Presentation video** (asr-000447)  
creating jobs and livelihood opportunities,

**[00:22:24.200] Presentation video** (asr-000448)  
preserving Filipino weaving traditions,

**[00:22:26.900] Presentation video** (asr-000449)  
and driving regional economic growth,

**[00:22:28.640] Presentation video** (asr-000450)  
through innovation.

**[00:22:30.180] Presentation video** (asr-000451)  
The Natural Textile Fiber Innovation Hubs

**[00:22:32.780] Presentation video** (asr-000452)  
support local processing of indigenous fibers,

**[00:22:35.580] Presentation video** (asr-000453)  
while Natural Dyes Hubs

**[00:22:36.980] Presentation video** (asr-000454)  
promote sustainable, locally sourced colorants.

**[00:22:40.260] Presentation video** (asr-000455)  
The Regional Yarn Production and Innovation Centers,

**[00:22:43.200] Presentation video** (asr-000456)  
or RIPEC,

**[00:22:44.040] Presentation video** (asr-000457)  
build regional capacity for yarn processing,

**[00:22:46.280] Presentation video** (asr-000458)  
while regional hubs for innovative handloom weaving

**[00:22:49.140] Presentation video** (asr-000459)  
equip local weavers with improved tools,

**[00:22:51.820] Presentation video** (asr-000460)  
technologies, and skills.

**[00:22:53.880] Presentation video** (asr-000461)  
The Wealth Protection Pillar

**[00:22:55.260] Presentation video** (asr-000462)  
strengthens resilience through STI,

**[00:22:57.740] Presentation video** (asr-000463)  
with the following key accomplishments

**[00:22:59.780] Presentation video** (asr-000464)  
achieved in 2025.

**[00:23:02.120] Presentation video** (asr-000465)  
Improving monitoring and warning systems

**[00:23:04.280] Presentation video** (asr-000466)  
to reduce risks from climate, weather, and other hazards.

**[00:23:08.320] Presentation video** (asr-000467)  
These initiatives enhance the country's capacity

**[00:23:10.640] Presentation video** (asr-000468)  
for early warning, climate monitoring,

**[00:23:13.220] Presentation video** (asr-000469)  
informed decision-making, and disaster risk reduction.

**[00:23:16.960] Presentation video** (asr-000470)  
For 2025-2026, DOST Pagasa established

**[00:23:21.180] Presentation video** (asr-000471)  
one Doppler radar in Iloilo,

**[00:23:24.040] Presentation video** (asr-000472)  
one fixed X-band radar,

**[00:23:25.660] Presentation video** (asr-000473)  
and two additional field weather stations,

**[00:23:27.740] Presentation video** (asr-000474)  
while maintaining its existing network

**[00:23:30.040] Presentation video** (asr-000475)  
of operational radar and weather monitoring facilities nationwide.

**[00:23:34.120] Presentation video** (asr-000476)  
These physical monitoring systems

**[00:23:35.980] Presentation video** (asr-000477)  
are complemented by digital innovations

**[00:23:38.020] Presentation video** (asr-000478)  
that improve access to timely climate information.

**[00:23:41.360] Presentation video** (asr-000479)  
These include the IHEAT map

**[00:23:43.060] Presentation video** (asr-000480)  
for heat index monitoring and prediction,

**[00:23:45.640] Presentation video** (asr-000481)  
PANAHON for nationwide hydrometeorological observations,

**[00:23:49.460] Presentation video** (asr-000482)  
the CMIP-6 climate information map,

**[00:23:52.200] Presentation video** (asr-000483)  
and the ENSO Assessment Simplified, or EASY, tool.

**[00:23:55.620] Presentation video** (asr-000484)  
The DOST also strengthens the network of data,

**[00:23:57.740] Presentation video** (asr-000485)  
including disaster monitoring and early warning systems

**[00:24:00.560] Presentation video** (asr-000486)  
to reduce risks and protect lives and communities.

**[00:24:04.120] Presentation video** (asr-000487)  
From 2025-2026,

**[00:24:06.820] Presentation video** (asr-000488)  
monitoring networks were expanded

**[00:24:08.380] Presentation video** (asr-000489)  
with 8 seismic stations,

**[00:24:10.380] Presentation video** (asr-000490)  
3 volcano seismic stations,

**[00:24:12.440] Presentation video** (asr-000491)  
3 tsunami sea-level stations,

**[00:24:14.440] Presentation video** (asr-000492)  
and 21 community tsunami alerting stations,

**[00:24:17.720] Presentation video** (asr-000493)  
strengthening real-time hazard detection nationwide.

**[00:24:21.440] Presentation video** (asr-000494)  
DOST also updated 969 municipal city-scale

**[00:24:25.560] Presentation video** (asr-000495)  
and 44 provincial-scale earth-scale systems,

**[00:24:27.740] Presentation video** (asr-000496)  
as well as 16 volcano hazard maps.

**[00:24:31.640] Presentation video** (asr-000497)  
Together, these expanded monitoring networks

**[00:24:34.120] Presentation video** (asr-000498)  
and updated hazard maps

**[00:24:35.480] Presentation video** (asr-000499)  
provide timely, science-based information

**[00:24:38.000] Presentation video** (asr-000500)  
for early warning, disaster preparedness,

**[00:24:40.660] Presentation video** (asr-000501)  
and informed land-use planning,

**[00:24:42.520] Presentation video** (asr-000502)  
helping protect lives, livelihoods, and communities.

**[00:24:46.040] Presentation video** (asr-000503)  
On strengthening capacities

**[00:24:47.420] Presentation video** (asr-000504)  
for local disaster risk reduction and management,

**[00:24:50.600] Presentation video** (asr-000505)  
the DOST also developed science-based tools

**[00:24:53.500] Presentation video** (asr-000506)  
and platforms for local disaster risk reduction and management

**[00:24:56.720] Presentation video** (asr-000507)  
and climate resistance,

**[00:24:57.740] Presentation video** (asr-000508)  
that turn data into actionable information,

**[00:25:01.320] Presentation video** (asr-000509)  
helping communities plan smarter,

**[00:25:03.340] Presentation video** (asr-000510)  
prepare better,

**[00:25:04.240] Presentation video** (asr-000511)  
and respond faster to disasters

**[00:25:06.160] Presentation video** (asr-000512)  
and climate-related risks.

**[00:25:08.400] Presentation video** (asr-000513)  
To build local DRRM capacity,

**[00:25:10.940] Presentation video** (asr-000514)  
68 provinces nationwide

**[00:25:12.700] Presentation video** (asr-000515)  
have been trained in using Redis software

**[00:25:14.740] Presentation video** (asr-000516)  
for hazard modeling, risk assessment,

**[00:25:16.980] Presentation video** (asr-000517)  
and disaster preparedness planning.

**[00:25:19.500] Presentation video** (asr-000518)  
DOST also developed PlanSmart for Safe Schools

**[00:25:22.660] Presentation video** (asr-000519)  
to support resilient educational infrastructure

**[00:25:25.360] Presentation video** (asr-000520)  
and PlanSmart for Sustainable Human Services,

**[00:25:27.740] Presentation video** (asr-000521)  
which is a new technology

**[00:25:29.500] Presentation video** (asr-000522)  
for the development of safe and resilient communities.

**[00:25:31.140] Presentation video** (asr-000523)  
The Honda integrated platform,

**[00:25:33.760] Presentation video** (asr-000524)  
launched in March 2026,

**[00:25:35.680] Presentation video** (asr-000525)  
provides government agencies,

**[00:25:37.280] Presentation video** (asr-000526)  
LGUs, and the public

**[00:25:38.660] Presentation video** (asr-000527)  
with a centralized source

**[00:25:40.160] Presentation video** (asr-000528)  
of real-time disaster preparedness

**[00:25:42.060] Presentation video** (asr-000529)  
and response information management.

**[00:25:44.420] Presentation video** (asr-000530)  
We also have the Smart Flood Management Approaches Framework,

**[00:25:48.200] Presentation video** (asr-000531)  
which organizes our smart flood management initiatives

**[00:25:50.940] Presentation video** (asr-000532)  
into three functions,

**[00:25:52.780] Presentation video** (asr-000533)  
Observe, Assess, and Solve,

**[00:25:55.080] Presentation video** (asr-000534)  
covering weather, groundwater,

**[00:25:57.740] Presentation video** (asr-000535)  
river, and urban flooding,

**[00:25:58.980] Presentation video** (asr-000536)  
coastal hazards, and landslides.

**[00:26:01.940] Presentation video** (asr-000537)  
The integrated flood management

**[00:26:03.620] Presentation video** (asr-000538)  
combines engineering,

**[00:26:04.900] Presentation video** (asr-000539)  
nature-based solutions,

**[00:26:06.380] Presentation video** (asr-000540)  
geospatial information,

**[00:26:08.100] Presentation video** (asr-000541)  
and public engagement.

**[00:26:09.860] Presentation video** (asr-000542)  
Through these initiatives,

**[00:26:11.060] Presentation video** (asr-000543)  
the DOST equips government agencies,

**[00:26:13.620] Presentation video** (asr-000544)  
LGUs, and communities

**[00:26:15.460] Presentation video** (asr-000545)  
with the science and technologies needed

**[00:26:17.780] Presentation video** (asr-000546)  
to make informed investments

**[00:26:19.260] Presentation video** (asr-000547)  
and build safer, more resilient communities.

**[00:26:22.620] Presentation video** (asr-000548)  
The Sustainability Pillar advances

**[00:26:24.820] Presentation video** (asr-000549)  
environmental protection and conservation

**[00:26:27.260] Presentation video** (asr-000550)  
through STI,

**[00:26:28.300] Presentation video** (asr-000551)  
and builds on the gains of existing STI investments

**[00:26:31.540] Presentation video** (asr-000552)  
by promoting long-term, evidence-based solutions

**[00:26:34.500] Presentation video** (asr-000553)  
that support environmental resilience,

**[00:26:36.700] Presentation video** (asr-000554)  
sustainable development, and inclusive growth.

**[00:26:39.260] Presentation video** (asr-000555)  
The following are key accomplishments achieved in 2025.

**[00:26:43.060] Presentation video** (asr-000556)  
Intensifying Environmental Sustainability

**[00:26:46.380] Presentation video** (asr-000557)  
The Department developed technologies

**[00:26:48.740] Presentation video** (asr-000558)  
for enhanced water quality monitoring,

**[00:26:50.740] Presentation video** (asr-000559)  
cost-effective and low-power vessel tracking

**[00:26:53.620] Presentation video** (asr-000560)  
for small fishing boats using AIS,

**[00:26:55.940] Presentation video** (asr-000561)  
and surveillance systems,

**[00:26:57.260] Presentation video** (asr-000562)  
and near real-time monitoring

**[00:26:59.420] Presentation video** (asr-000563)  
of land use changes and compliance

**[00:27:01.540] Presentation video** (asr-000564)  
with LGU land use plans and zoning ordinances.

**[00:27:05.500] Presentation video** (asr-000565)  
The DOST is developing

**[00:27:07.100] Presentation video** (asr-000566)  
an AI-powered wood identification application

**[00:27:10.260] Presentation video** (asr-000567)  
to enable faster field verification

**[00:27:12.460] Presentation video** (asr-000568)  
of wood species,

**[00:27:13.780] Presentation video** (asr-000569)  
helping strengthen timber traceability,

**[00:27:16.060] Presentation video** (asr-000570)  
forest protection,

**[00:27:17.220] Presentation video** (asr-000571)  
and sustainable resource management.

**[00:27:19.700] Presentation video** (asr-000572)  
By combining traditional wood identification

**[00:27:22.500] Presentation video** (asr-000573)  
with DNA-based methods,

**[00:27:24.540] Presentation video** (asr-000574)  
DOST is developing specialized extractions,

**[00:27:27.260] Presentation video** (asr-000575)  
extraction protocols,

**[00:27:28.420] Presentation video** (asr-000576)  
and a Philippine Timber DNA database

**[00:27:31.100] Presentation video** (asr-000577)  
to strengthen timber traceability,

**[00:27:33.260] Presentation video** (asr-000578)  
support species conservation,

**[00:27:35.180] Presentation video** (asr-000579)  
and help deter illegal logging.

**[00:27:37.700] Presentation video** (asr-000580)  
In the long term,

**[00:27:38.740] Presentation video** (asr-000581)  
this initiative aims to build

**[00:27:40.300] Presentation video** (asr-000582)  
a more comprehensive scientific basis

**[00:27:42.860] Presentation video** (asr-000583)  
for protecting and sustainably managing

**[00:27:45.140] Presentation video** (asr-000584)  
Philippine tree species.

**[00:27:47.300] Presentation video** (asr-000585)  
The DOST supports ecosystem rehabilitation,

**[00:27:50.540] Presentation video** (asr-000586)  
including technologies for monitoring radiation in corals,

**[00:27:54.020] Presentation video** (asr-000587)  
and improving the recovery and rehabilitation

**[00:27:56.420] Presentation video** (asr-000588)  
of damaged coral reefs.

**[00:27:58.540] Presentation video** (asr-000589)  
On establishing smart and sustainable communities,

**[00:28:01.660] Presentation video** (asr-000590)  
the DOST promotes data-driven technologies

**[00:28:04.580] Presentation video** (asr-000591)  
and innovative mobility solutions.

**[00:28:07.060] Presentation video** (asr-000592)  
These initiatives help build smarter,

**[00:28:09.140] Presentation video** (asr-000593)  
cleaner, and more resilient communities

**[00:28:11.580] Presentation video** (asr-000594)  
while using science, technology, and innovation

**[00:28:14.460] Presentation video** (asr-000595)  
to support sustainable development.

**[00:28:17.420] Presentation video** (asr-000596)  
To improve public service delivery

**[00:28:19.220] Presentation video** (asr-000597)  
through cleaner, more efficient,

**[00:28:20.860] Presentation video** (asr-000598)  
and data-driven waste management,

**[00:28:22.860] Presentation video** (asr-000599)  
digital tools,

**[00:28:23.900] Presentation video** (asr-000600)  
and mountain-focused innovation platforms,

**[00:28:26.260] Presentation video** (asr-000601)  
DOST implemented the Smart and Sustainable Communities Program,

**[00:28:30.220] Presentation video** (asr-000602)  
SSCP.

**[00:28:32.220] Presentation video** (asr-000603)  
For smart urban planning,

**[00:28:33.820] Presentation video** (asr-000604)  
modern geospatial and collaborative solutions

**[00:28:36.380] Presentation video** (asr-000605)  
for the development of smart regions,

**[00:28:38.260] Presentation video** (asr-000606)  
smart metro,

**[00:28:39.420] Presentation video** (asr-000607)  
uses geospatial modeling to support better planning.

**[00:28:42.940] Presentation video** (asr-000608)  
For smart mobility and logistics,

**[00:28:44.980] Presentation video** (asr-000609)  
alpha development of vehicle-to-everything,

**[00:28:47.340] Presentation video** (asr-000610)  
V2X transceivers for user scenarios,

**[00:28:50.180] Presentation video** (asr-000611)  
Adventis develops vehicle-to-everything technology

**[00:28:53.260] Presentation video** (asr-000612)  
for connected transportation.

Urban sustainability.

**[00:29:20.060] Presentation video** (asr-000621)  
Empowering Cagayan de Oro City

**[00:29:22.060] Presentation video** (asr-000622)  
with smart decision support for waste-to-energy,

**[00:29:24.660] Presentation video** (asr-000623)  
SWEEP CDOC,

for waste-to-energy management.

DOST advances circular economy

Other innovations include

biodegradable packaging,

from renewable resources

and carbon microspheres

from polypropylene waste

On improving access

to clean and green energy,

the DOST developed

alternative energy initiatives

supporting renewable energy.

**[00:30:14.420] Presentation video** (asr-000650)  
These initiatives

**[00:30:15.100] Presentation video** (asr-000651)  
make clean energy

**[00:30:16.140] Presentation video** (asr-000652)  
more accessible,

**[00:30:17.360] Presentation video** (asr-000653)  
scalable,

helping communities

To harness solar energy,

DOST develops

solar thermal applications

and uses solar PV

For energy storage,

The department also established

which advances reliable

DOST also promotes

waste-to-energy

and biomass solutions,

including waste-to-energy systems,

**[00:37:34.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000886)  
Doon sa ano, of course, one of the hottest issues now is the Pax Silica Initiative.

**[00:37:40.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000887)  
As the primary government agency in the Philippines,

**[00:37:42.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000888)  
tasked with providing central direction, leadership, and coordination of all scientific and technological efforts in the country.

**[00:37:50.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000889)  
What is the official position of DOST?

**[00:37:55.680] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000890)  
The principle of DOST for any development is that it must be sustainable.

**[00:38:00.720] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000891)  
So we have to make sure that the development plans are aligned to what we want to create more jobs and opportunities.

**[00:38:09.320] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000892)  
While our safeguards should be focused on the environmental and other socioeconomic concerns that we have.

**[00:38:19.460] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000893)  
Basically,

**[00:38:20.040] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000894)  
based on what I have heard from the direct explanation of the program,

**[00:38:27.440] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000895)  
the Pax Silica is a global program.

**[00:38:30.560] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000896)  
But essentially, the component in the Philippines is a very small part of it.

**[00:38:38.340] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000897)  
Where the real intent of the component in Clark is to have an advanced manufacturing hub

**[00:38:46.960] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000898)  
for accessories that will be related.

**[00:38:50.040] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000899)  
In the future,

**[00:38:51.660] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000900)  
to chips for other accessories that will be used for data centers or AI hubs.

**[00:38:58.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000901)  
But those will not be in the Clark area.

**[00:39:02.840] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000902)  
Second, on the minerals processing, it is not certain yet as this will be negotiated.

**[00:39:09.080] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000903)  
As to what minerals, the state of minerals, the critical minerals that they will use.

**[00:39:15.380] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000904)  
Because for sure, one should not mine and transport it.

**[00:39:20.040] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000905)  
But one should not mine and transport it to the area and process it economically.

**[00:39:25.040] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000906)  
And therefore, those still have to be discussed.

**[00:39:27.420] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000907)  
So we have to make sure that the development in the area will really promote the economy of the country

**[00:39:35.020] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000908)  
while making sure that the environmental concerns and the social concerns within the area will be addressed.

**[00:39:41.140] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000909)  
Thank you.

**[00:39:41.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000910)

**[00:39:45.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000911)  
Good afternoon.

**[00:39:47.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000912)  
Hopefully, I'd just like to ask.

**[00:39:50.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000913)  
Also, if you are based on the initial assessment with the creation of this venture,

**[00:39:55.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000914)  
the Pax Silica, outweigh the severe environmental risk,

**[00:39:59.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000915)  
mayroon ba ako yung study na rin?

**[00:40:00.780] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000916)  
Well, essentially, if they will be operating an industrial hub,

**[00:40:05.680] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000917)  
we have many industrial hubs in the Philippines like in Cavite, Depeza.

**[00:40:09.980] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000918)  
So we are very, very strict on our environmental laws.

**[00:40:13.400] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000919)  
And this must be, of course, be led by the DNR.

**[00:40:17.720] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000920)  
So marami na po tayong experience.

**[00:40:19.140] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000921)  
And pagdating sa environmental law, dapat very strict.

**[00:40:22.980] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000922)  
At wala namang naging issue out of the PESA zones that we have.

**[00:40:28.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000923)  
Okay, thank you.

**[00:40:30.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000924)  
Doon lang po sa ano.

**[00:40:32.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000925)  
August this year, DBM released 1 billion for continued implementation expansion

**[00:40:36.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000926)  
of the nationwide operation assessment of hazards or Project NOAA.

**[00:40:41.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000927)  
According to the UP Resilience Institute, it's a good start, but I think it's not,

**[00:40:45.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000928)  
we feel it's not enough.

**[00:40:47.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000929)  
May we know if Project NOAA has,

**[00:40:49.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000930)  
budget allocation under your department or any of your attached agency

**[00:40:52.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000931)  
to complement the proposed budget, if any, under the UP system?

**[00:40:57.720] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000932)  
NOAA is operated by the University of the Philippines.

**[00:41:01.580] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000933)  
And we have other disaster and climate-related programs

**[00:41:05.840] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000934)  
that would complement the provision of information and knowledge

**[00:41:11.260] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000935)  
to various stakeholders, including government agencies, industries,

**[00:41:16.020] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000936)  
and more importantly, communities.

**[00:41:18.060] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000937)  
So we have developed...

**[00:41:19.140] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000938)  
We have developed many programs and web applications

**[00:41:22.260] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000939)  
and even phone applications that would enable people to have access to data

**[00:41:29.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000940)  
from the different mandated organizations po.

**[00:41:33.300] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000941)  
And just to separate,

**[00:41:36.000] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000942)  
PHIVOX would essentially be the one just providing all the geologic information.

**[00:41:41.220] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000943)  
Wala pong counterpart dyan.

**[00:41:42.700] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000944)  
Sila po talaga yung mandated.

**[00:41:44.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000945)  
Okay, thank you, Secretary.

**[00:41:46.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000946)  
I'll just have three...

**[00:41:49.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000947)  
main issues for budget utilization.

**[00:41:53.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000948)  
Of course, that's the reason I'm here.

**[00:41:55.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000949)  
The DOSD is requesting for a 30.47 billion new appropriations

**[00:41:59.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000950)  
for fiscal year 2027.

**[00:42:02.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000951)  
Yet, records show that your unused appropriations

**[00:42:05.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000952)  
surged by nearly 80% to 2.85 billion in 2025.

**[00:42:09.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000953)  
Given by severe disbursement challenges in frontline agents like PAGASA and PNRI,

**[00:42:15.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000954)  
why is there...

**[00:42:19.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000955)  
a request of a much bigger funding compared to 2025

**[00:42:24.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000956)  
kung hindi naman nare-resolve yung unused appropriations?

**[00:42:30.620] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000957)  
Of course, different agencies would have specific needs.

**[00:42:34.580] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000958)  
And if the agency is cited like PHIVOX and PNRI,

**[00:42:37.940] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000959)  
these are usually capital outlays that sometimes would have procurement delays

**[00:42:43.380] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000960)  
because some of the equipment are not off the shelf.

**[00:42:45.940] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000961)  
So you have to wait a little bit sometimes around two years.

**[00:42:49.140] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000962)  
So pagdating po naman sa ibang ahensya,

**[00:42:51.720] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000963)  
we really need the budget because based on our experience,

**[00:42:55.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000964)  
we can create more impact that would translate to more livelihood,

**[00:42:59.700] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000965)  
more efficient processes.

**[00:43:01.480] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000966)  
And so yung pong minsan utilization issue is more on the capital outlay.

**[00:43:07.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000967)  
In 2025, the DOSD accumulated 2.85 billion in unused appropriations

**[00:43:12.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000968)  
up from 1.57 billion.

**[00:43:14.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000969)  
In 2024, PAGASA alone accounted for 1.45 billion.

**[00:43:19.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000970)  
1.2 billion.

**[00:43:20.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000971)  
And the OSEC left 693.2 million unspent.

**[00:43:24.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000972)  
My question is,

**[00:43:26.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000973)  
Sir Chair, ano-ano ang mga proyekto o programa na nagkaroon ng mapagsubok?

**[00:43:31.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000974)  
O ba't ganito kalaki na umabot yung unused appropriations natin?

**[00:43:35.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000975)  
At bakano ang nabalik sa National Treasury?

**[00:43:37.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000976)  
Pasensya na, we have to ask now because of our very, very limited fiscal space,

**[00:43:43.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000977)  
we have to make sure that all our appropriations are well spent.

**[00:43:49.140] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000978)  
May, Mr. Chair, may I ask the Administrator of PAGASA to respond to the specific question on PAGASA?

**[00:43:55.500] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000979)  
Thank you.

**[00:43:56.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000980)  
Yes, please proceed.

**[00:43:59.320] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-000981)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[00:44:01.180] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-000982)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary, for this opportunity.

**[00:44:04.620] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-000983)  
As for the 2025 budget, the unutilized fund,

**[00:44:10.660] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-000984)  
actually, part of that were given additional budget out of the National Disaster Risk Reduction Management Council Fund,

**[00:44:19.200] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-000985)  
supposedly to purchase three doppler radars.

**[00:44:25.240] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-000986)  
But at the moment, the procurement process is ongoing.

**[00:44:30.100] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-000987)  
In fact, most of the equipment are already awarded.

**[00:44:34.400] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-000988)  
And we're confident before the year ends, this will be utilized or obligated.

**[00:44:40.720] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-000989)  
Thank you, Chair.

**[00:44:44.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000990)  
So, on target, end of the year, it will be fully utilized.

**[00:44:48.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000991)  
Yung mga equipment.

**[00:44:49.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000992)  
Natin, it's already operational.

**[00:44:51.460] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-000993)  
Yes, yes.

**[00:44:53.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-000994)  
Yes, sir.

**[00:44:54.000] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000995)  
So, for sometimes the OSEC money will be...

**[00:44:59.900] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000996)  
a continuing allocation for the following year

**[00:45:03.020] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000997)  
because sometimes we have challenges on the requirement

**[00:45:07.800] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000998)  
for the implementing organizations to really submit their liquidation

**[00:45:13.420] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-000999)  
for many of their previous proposals.

**[00:45:16.220] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001000)  
So we have to make sure that they observe that.

**[00:45:20.360] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001001)  
And so sometimes we actually use it the following year,

**[00:45:24.820] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001002)  
but trust assured, Mr. Chair, that these are fully utilized

**[00:45:28.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001003)  
by providing additional funding for new proposals.

**[00:45:33.040] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001004)  
Our application for Granada 8 is 99% for the continuing.

**[00:45:39.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001005)  
Just to clarify, the OSTs overall disbursement rate slipped

**[00:45:44.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001006)  
from 83.8% in 2024 to 79.8% in 2025.

**[00:45:51.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001007)  
Pag-assess disbursement rate plunged to 47.9%,

**[00:45:55.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001008)  
down from 71.1% in 2020.

**[00:45:58.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001009)  
And PNRI disbursed only 34.4%.

**[00:46:04.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001010)  
May I ask the two agencies to respond?

**[00:46:17.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001011)  
First, Pag-asa and then PNRI.

**[00:46:19.360] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001012)  
Essentially, it's related to the capital outlay.

**[00:46:22.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001013)  
Yes, please. Pag-asa muna and then PNRI.

**[00:46:25.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001014)  
What measures, interventions?

**[00:46:28.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001015)  
Are being, will be done, no?

**[00:46:32.080] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001016)  
Yes, thank you.

**[00:46:33.280] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001017)  
Once again, Your Honor and the Secretary.

**[00:46:37.940] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001018)  
Interventions include fast tracking the procurement process.

**[00:46:43.140] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001019)  
Of course, the equipment involved is sophisticated

**[00:46:47.160] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001020)  
and is manufactured overseas.

**[00:46:50.620] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001021)  
It takes time before, of course, it is to be installed.

**[00:46:55.000] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001022)  
But the Vision Awards Committee,

**[00:46:58.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001023)  
we have already done the fast tracking.

**[00:47:00.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001024)  
And at the moment, in fact, as I've mentioned,

**[00:47:03.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001025)  
we have already secured the awarded contracts.

**[00:47:08.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001026)  
And of course, the remaining components will be closely monitored

**[00:47:13.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001027)  
until, since 2025, this 2025 budget,

**[00:47:18.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001028)  
and this is continuing until December this year,

**[00:47:21.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001029)  
we're confident it will be utilized.

**[00:47:24.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001030)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[00:47:25.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001031)  
Thank you.

**[00:47:26.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001032)  
Sa PNRI po.

**[00:47:28.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001033)  
Good afternoon, Chairs, Your Honorable Chairs.

**[00:47:32.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001034)  
For the justification for the low utilization,

**[00:47:38.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001035)  
it's because of the nuclear medicine project.

**[00:47:41.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001036)  
The reason for this low obligation or low utilization

**[00:47:47.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001037)  
is first is the procurement planning needed to be revisited

**[00:47:50.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001038)  
to align with the actual project implementation progress

**[00:47:54.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001039)  
and to create a more efficient budget utilization.

**[00:47:57.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001040)  
It's because of some procurement packages

**[00:47:59.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001041)  
were therefore not yet sufficiently matured or procurement ready.

**[00:48:03.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001042)  
The second one is the project undertook a more thorough review

**[00:48:07.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001043)  
of its technical and procurement documents.

**[00:48:10.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001044)  
And the last one, the third one,

**[00:48:13.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001045)  
is the regulatory legal requirements as being addressed.

**[00:48:16.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001046)  
So our catch-up measures is the completion of the procurement documents,

**[00:48:20.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001047)  
the engineering plans, terms of references,

**[00:48:23.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001048)  
and the technical specifications and other details,

**[00:48:26.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001049)  
cost estimate will be expedited with the current target of completion

**[00:48:33.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001050)  
and submissions of major building requirements.

**[00:48:36.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001051)  
Secondly is the resolutions of the legal and implementation concerns

**[00:48:40.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001052)  
of which the PNRI is coordinating with appropriate legal authorities

**[00:48:44.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001053)  
and strengthening internal legal support

**[00:48:47.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001054)  
to facilitate timely resolutions of issues

**[00:48:49.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001055)  
that have been affected the project itself.

**[00:48:52.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001056)  
The implementation of the revised project roadmap

**[00:48:56.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001057)  
The revised roadmap is being used to utilize a clear sequence

**[00:49:00.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001058)  
for technical procurement and legal and implementation activities

**[00:49:04.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001059)  
to be bring to the major procurement packages

**[00:49:07.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001060)  
to the required level of readiness.

**[00:49:10.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001061)  
So right now po, we are now working expediting this process

**[00:49:16.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001062)  
to make sure that the utilization for the nuclear medicine project

**[00:49:20.540] PNRI Deputy Director Maria Teresa A. Salabit** (asr-001063)  
will be expedited.

**[00:49:22.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001064)  
Thank you.

**[00:49:23.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001065)  
Mr. Chair,

**[00:49:25.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001066)  
at the time when the Philippines faces escalating climate catastrophes,

**[00:49:31.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001067)  
proposed fiscal year 2027 budget slashes

**[00:49:34.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001068)  
Pag-ASA's weather and climate forecasting and warning

**[00:49:38.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001069)  
programmed by 53.4% from 1.08 billion to 707.9 million.

**[00:49:45.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001070)

**[00:49:48.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001071)  
what is the position of DOST and PAGASA on this?

**[00:49:52.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001072)  
What will be the effect of this?

**[00:49:54.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001073)  
What would be the effect of this budget cut

**[00:49:57.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001074)  
in the operation of Pag-ASA?

**[00:49:59.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001075)  
May I ask the Administrator?

**[00:50:01.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001076)  
But before he answers, Your Honor,

**[00:50:03.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001077)  
essentially, many of the decrease in the current proposed 27 budget

**[00:50:09.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001078)

**[00:50:16.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001079)

**[00:50:17.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001080)  
But I'll ask Administrator Cervantes to give the details.

**[00:50:22.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001081)  
Thank you.

**[00:50:23.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001082)  
And thank you, Secretary.

**[00:50:32.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001084)

**[00:50:36.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001085)

**[00:50:41.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001086)

**[00:50:45.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001087)

**[00:51:02.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001089)

**[00:51:05.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001090)

**[00:51:16.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001093)

which includes one of the locally funded projects,

**[00:51:27.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001095)

**[00:51:31.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001096)  
But anyway, this is continuing for next year.

**[00:51:36.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001097)  
And in the succeeding years,

**[00:51:40.540] PAGASA Administrator Nathaniel T. Servando** (asr-001098)

**[00:51:43.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001099)  
If I may add, Your Honor,

**[00:51:45.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001100)  
essentially, some of the things that we do at DOST

**[00:51:49.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001101)  
is to partner Pagano,

**[00:51:51.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001102)  
with other DOST agencies

**[00:51:54.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001103)  
like the Advanced Science and Technology Institute,

**[00:51:57.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001104)  
and other international partners.

**[00:51:59.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001105)  
For example, he was mentioning about the use of AI

**[00:52:02.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001106)  
in weather and climate forecasting.

**[00:52:05.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001107)  
And so, although it is not reflected in their budget,

**[00:52:08.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001108)  
because it's a research activity,

**[00:52:10.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001109)  
we provide the funding

**[00:52:12.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001110)  
that would contribute to the improvement of their forecasting.

**[00:52:16.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001111)  
So the target for that particular project

**[00:52:18.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001112)  
is to advance forecasting from five days

**[00:52:21.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001113)  
to 14 days,

**[00:52:22.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001114)  
and the compute time,

**[00:52:24.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001115)  
which takes three hours now,

**[00:52:26.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001116)  
to every 15 minutes.

**[00:52:28.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001117)  
So that everyone would understand,

**[00:52:32.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001118)  
DOST provides R&D money

**[00:52:34.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001119)  
to improve the operational forecasting capability

**[00:52:37.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001120)  
of PAGASA.

**[00:52:39.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001121)  
So that's why it's very important

**[00:52:41.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001122)  
that we really look into how

**[00:52:43.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001123)  
further we can improve PAGASA

**[00:52:45.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001124)  
by integrating efforts of other DOST agencies

**[00:52:48.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-001125)  
or the universities.

**[00:57:26.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001243)

**[00:57:30.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001246)

**[00:57:31.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001247)

**[00:57:32.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001248)

**[00:57:33.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001249)

**[00:57:43.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001253)

**[00:57:44.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001254)

**[00:57:45.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001255)  
ng 9 a.m.

**[00:57:47.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001256)

**[00:57:53.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001259)

**[00:58:02.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001264)

**[00:58:05.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001266)

**[00:58:10.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001268)  
Mr. Chairman.

**[00:58:11.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001269)

**[00:58:12.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001270)

**[00:58:15.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001271)

**[00:58:16.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001272)

**[00:58:19.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001273)

**[00:58:20.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001274)

**[00:58:22.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001275)  
Okay na sana yun eh,

**[00:58:23.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001276)

**[00:58:25.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001277)

**[01:16:11.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-002132)

**[01:16:41.380] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-002137)

Sige po, Secretary.

**[01:17:46.180] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002148)  
Thank you, Senator, for that very good suggestion.

**[01:17:50.300] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002149)  
In fact, that is the real purpose of...

**[01:17:52.800] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002150)  
the four strategic pillars that we mentioned and the mantra Agham Ramdam.

**[01:17:59.900] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002151)  
For example, in the textile industry business, we have the producers of cotton and fabric from pineapple, banana, abaca, or kawayan.

**[01:18:17.720] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002152)  
And then we have the weaver.

**[01:18:20.480] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002153)  
So if you look at the ecosystem...

**[01:18:22.800] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002154)  
The pain point there is that, okay, they produce, then somebody would weave, pero walang gumagawa ng sinulit.

**[01:18:29.760] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002155)  
And so we decided, through PTI, to develop the Regional Yarn Production and Innovation Center.

**[01:18:36.140] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002156)  
And that's solved the problem now.

**[01:18:38.480] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002157)  
Yung gana ng mga cotton farmer, gusto nang magproduce nang magproduce kasi meron ng bumibili.

**[01:18:45.500] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002158)  
So the Batangas Egg Producers Cooperative is another one.

**[01:18:48.820] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002159)  
Their problem was...

**[01:18:49.820] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002160)  
Yung mga maninipis ang shell, madaling mabasag, tinatapon na lang yan.

**[01:18:56.540] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002161)  
But because of technology, they can now preserve it.

**[01:19:00.300] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002162)  
And with the ITDI technology, yung spray drying technique, you can actually create powders out of it.

**[01:19:07.460] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002163)  
And the shelf life will be at least two years.

**[01:19:10.180] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002164)  
So, yun po.

**[01:19:11.500] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002165)  
Tomorrow, I'll visit another place.

**[01:19:13.880] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002166)  
It's called NutriDense Corporation.

**[01:19:16.300] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002167)

**[01:19:19.820] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002168)

**[01:19:23.800] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002169)  
And the engagement is continuous.

**[01:19:26.720] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002170)  
The real strategy now, Your Honor, is to make sure that we actually evaluate the innovation ecosystem.

**[01:19:34.880] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002171)  
There are producers, there are small enterprises, micro-enterprises.

**[01:19:39.700] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002172)  
Then we have the consumer, but no one would...

**[01:19:43.100] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002173)  
In the middle.

**[01:19:43.860] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002174)  
So we wanted to make sure that we have some firms in the middle.

**[01:19:49.820] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002175)  
Before I came in, the setup would only provide assistance to the enterprise two times only.

**[01:19:59.800] DOST Secretary Renato U. Solidum Jr.** (asr-002176)
